- **speceyes-speculative-agentic-acceleration**: Accelerate agentic multimodal LLMs via speculative execution without sacrificing accuracy. - **special-fixer**: Fix items from a review checklist (REVIEW_ITEMS.md) produced by special-review. Each implementer verifies the claim before acting — if they disagree they mark [~] wont-fix. After implementers finish, merges the worktree branches back into main, dispatching implementers to resolve any conflicts. Reads LEARNINGS.md from the companion review directory and provides it to implementers.
